# 第三类总进度与 CI 分册索引：Legado Runtime

> **第三类唯一总入口。** 每次第三类任务必须先读 `docs/required/00-MASTER-WORKFLOW.md`，再读本文件；随后只读当前任务需要的 CI 分册或技术历史段。所有工作只在 `integration/legado-upstream-runtime`，禁止写入 main。
> 固定行为基准：`LegadoTeam/legado@330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a`；许可证 GPL-3.0。

## 1. 第三类总目标与不可降低的要求

交付独立运行的坏坏猫增强客户端：坏坏猫始终是唯一产品外壳，保留原版搜索 UI、页面、导航、收藏/书架、净化、书签、阅读器、CSS/排版和交互；Legado 只提供完整书源运行能力，不搬入 Legado UI、书架产品流程或 Reader UI。

完整书源兼容必须以固定 SHA 为唯一行为基准，逐项覆盖并差分验证：

1. BookSource/Book/BookChapter/ReadConfig 等模型字段、默认值、null/missing、序列化和未知字段。
2. 全部书源规则类型及 AnalyzeRule、AnalyzeUrl、CSS、XPath、JSONPath、正则、替换、上下文和错误语义。
3. Rhino、全部书源相关 JsExtensions 声明/参数/返回值/overload、jsLib、Java/JS Bridge、SharedJsScope。
4. HTTP/GET/POST/HEAD、请求配置、响应对象、header/cookie、多值 header、重定向、超时、取消和错误传播。
5. Cookie、登录/loginUi/loginInfo、源变量、会话隔离、BaseSource 完整生命周期、WebView/浏览器/验证码等源脚本实际使用的回调。
6. 导入/导出、搜索/发现/分页、详情、目录/目录分页、正文/多页正文、批量正文、章节身份与 URL alias。
7. 章节缓存、SaveToken/Fence、CacheManager、缓存失效、数据持久化和重启恢复。
8. 多类型真实书源 E2E、脱敏录制回放、Android 集成、坏坏猫原 UI 回归、exact HEAD APK/报告/哈希和许可证验收。

**禁止：** selector-only、shadow DTO 冒充官方模型、同名简化 API、no-op、空返回、假数据/假成功、测试替身进入生产路径；不得用单条 Search→Detail→TOC→Content 成功、外部 Python 抓取、Debug APK 构建或 CI workflow success 宣称完整兼容。缺少证据的能力必须标为未实现/待验证/阻断。

## 2. 固定实施顺序

0. 固定上游、API/依赖差异盘点与 Runtime-3.0 架构门禁。
1. 数据模型、身份与序列化。
2. AnalyzeRule/AnalyzeUrl 和全部解析/替换语义。
3. Rhino、JsExtensions、BaseSource、JS 上下文和生命周期。
4. HTTP、Cookie、登录、请求/响应和安全边界。
5. 书源导入、搜索/发现、详情、目录、正文及统一编排。
6. 章节身份、URL alias、BatchContentContext 和多页/批量正文。
7. 章节缓存、token fence、CacheManager、失效和原子性。
8. 阅读进度、书签、历史、ReadConfig/ParagraphAnchor 与 Badcat 数据映射。
9. 五域事实源、单写者、事务、迁移、回滚、重启恢复。
10. 契约/架构测试、脱敏回放、多类型真实源、真机、APK 和发布验收。
11. 与第一、二类集成并完成最终发布验收。

不得越过前置门禁；架构选择服务于完整兼容性，不得为了维持旧 B′ 或 Android-free 边界而缩减上游语义。不得未经单写者、备份、dry-run、幂等、回滚和恢复验证就冻结 schema v1 或切换 Room/数据事实源。

## 3. 任务树与逐项验收要求

| 大类 | 必须覆盖的具体要求 | 核心验收 |
|---|---|---|
| 0 架构/治理 | 固定 SHA、GPL 来源、模块边界、API/依赖差异、退出方案 | 每项可追溯；架构 GO/NO-GO 有证据 |
| 1 模型/序列化 | BookSource、Book、BookChapter、Rule、ReadConfig、身份映射 | 字段/默认/null/未知字段/双形态序列化差分 |
| 2 规则/内容 | AnalyzeRule、AnalyzeUrl、CSS/XPath/JSON/正则、变量、替换、分页 | 固定上游逐项差分，无静默空结果 |
| 3 JS/生命周期 | Rhino、JsExtensions 全 API/overload、Bridge、BaseSource、jsLib、上下文 | API/ABI/行为/回调/取消/释放差分 |
| 4 网络/会话 | HTTP、请求/响应、重定向、Cookie、登录、源变量、安全 | header/cookie/错误/超时/取消/源隔离一致 |
| 5 业务链 | 导入导出、搜索/发现/分页、详情、目录、正文、统一编排 | 多类型书源真实链路和回放通过 |
| 6 章节身份 | (bookUrl, chapterIndex)、URL alias、旧链接、批量正文、排序 | 重复 URL 不混淆；歧义 fail closed |
| 7 缓存/持久化 | 正文缓存、SaveToken/Fence、CacheManager、失效、原子性、旧数据迁移 | 旧异步写不能覆盖新内容；重启/并发正确 |
| 8 阅读状态 | 进度、书签、历史、ReadConfig、ParagraphAnchor、收藏映射 | 单一权威状态、重启恢复、旧链接兼容 |
| 9 五域 PoC | BookSource/Book/BookChapter/Progress/Cache、事务、架构守卫、迁移回滚 | 每域唯一事实源/写入者；迁移可回滚 |
| 10 验收交付 | 单元/契约、架构、回放、真实源、真机、CI/APK/manifest/GPL | exact HEAD 对应完整证据，所有关键门禁通过 |

## 4. 当前总体进度（2026-11-24，依据原第三类清单与最新记录）

<badge>总体：部分实现 / 未验收</badge>

- **完整 Legado 书源兼容：未完成。** Stage-0 架构/依赖闭包审查仍 in_progress/not accepted；Stage-1 仍 NO-GO。
- **模型/序列化：部分实现且存在运行时字段损失。** 六类规则类与 Gson adapter 声明已对齐固定上游并有局部测试；但 host BookSource 仍是 JSON 字符串 DTO，RuleResolver 丢弃 ContentRule.maxBatchSize / ReviewRule.enabled 等非字符串字段，官方 BookSource/Book/BookChapter/ReadConfig 完整模型闭包未完成（§24.51–§24.52 已补充实体证据，运行时与数据所有权仍未闭合）。详见 §24.50。
- **规则/JS API：未完成。** 104-name JsExtensions/JsEncodeUtils 盘点已建立，但签名、返回对象、caller、动态分派和行为差分尚未全部闭合；已发现多项真实 ABI/行为缺口。
- **HTTP/响应：部分实现且有阻断。** Rhino response facade 与 pinned Jsoup Response 在 bodyAsBytes、header/multiHeaders、cookie、response lifecycle 等方面仍有差异。
- **浏览器/验证码：未完成。** webView、startBrowser、startBrowserAwait、getVerificationCode、showBrowser、openUrl 等有真实书源 caller；Badcat 原生 typed callback 方案需独立登记实施。
- **Stage-0 最新证据：** 详细审计 §24.42–§24.53 已补充导航/浏览器、WebView、书源状态清理、jsLib 共享库、AnalyzeRule/AnalyzeUrl 四条业务链、RuleDataInterface 变量作用域、规则/BookSource/Book/BookChapter 模型，以及 Cache/CacheManager/ACache/BookCacheInfo 存储和清理语义；完整上游运行闭包、类型化数据所有权、缓存差分和动态验证仍待闭合。详见 `docs/audits/legado-runtime-3.0-stage0-architecture-audit.md`。
- **业务链：局部实现。** Badcat host search/detail/TOC/content 编排存在，但不等于完整 BaseSource/session/规则执行生命周期。
- **章节身份：局部实现。** PoC-0.8/0.9 已将 index 带入主要内容边界并让旧 URL-only 只在唯一匹配时继续；完整 alias 候选与 BatchContentContext 未完成。
- **缓存：局部实现，未达兼容。** pinned Cache/CacheDao/CacheManager/ACache 的 Room + 50 MiB memory LRU + 文件 TTL 语义已审计（§24.53）；host 只有分离的 source KV/file cache 与 parsed chapter AtomicFile cache，generic CacheManager/ACache parity、完整 source/book mutation invalidation、跨实例 save-fence 与持久化差分仍未完成。
- **阅读状态/五域数据：未验收。** ReadConfig/ParagraphAnchor、单一事实源、单写者、跨域事务、迁移回滚和五域重启恢复未通过。
- **真实源/真机/发布：未验收。** 外部 Python 书源冒烟不算 Android + Legado Runtime E2E；真机、最终 APK 和发布门禁未完成。

## 4.1 Runtime-3.0 Stage-0 technical task registration — BookSource six rule models

- **Task ID:** R3-S0-MODEL-BOOKSOURCE-RULES-001
- **Status:** source-audit slice complete; runtime/model closure remains open.
- **Start HEAD:** e67add9c1c8bc4a031ef7a60e131c275c183fca2; branch `integration/legado-upstream-runtime`; upstream pin `330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a`.
- **Goal:** audit the six BookSource rule models (SearchRule, ExploreRule, BookInfoRule, TocRule, ContentRule, ReviewRule) and their JSON/Gson/serialization path against the pinned upstream; determine fields, nested objects, defaults, nullable/missing behavior, aliases, raw JSON retention and host codec loss.
- **Allowed files:** `docs/audits/legado-runtime-3.0-stage0-architecture-audit.md` and this overview only. Upstream and host source reads are evidence gathering; no source edits.
- **Callers / evidence:** locate all six upstream declarations, BookSource serialization/import/export and runtime accessors/callers; inspect host codec, source DTOs, rule resolver, fixtures and tests without changing them.
- **Acceptance:** source-path/line-backed field matrix for all six models; upstream-vs-host type/default/null/unknown-field/nested-value comparison; JSON string-vs-object handling and round-trip evidence; explicit confirmed facts vs static risks vs dynamic unverified items; duplicate check against existing audit sections.
- **Exclusions:** no production code, tests, Gradle, workflow, database/schema, UI or CI changes/runs; no claim of full model compatibility from raw JSON retention alone.
- **Result:** §24.50 records the six model field matrix, Gson/converter semantics, rawJson retention boundary, non-string field loss and Review caller gap. Next technical slice: inspect the outer pinned BookSource entity fields/defaults/identity/Room converter lifecycle against host catalog/DTO, without repeating six-rule findings.

## 5. 当前 CI 位置与分册规则

CI 审计按连续编号区间建立分册，每册最多 200 个 CI 编号，编号首尾包含且不得跨册重复。历史追溯已确认第三类 Legado Runtime 的 CI 至少从 #5 开始；#1–#4 暂无第三类关联证据。历史分册按以下顺序补建：
- 历史第 1 册：`docs/required/CI-0001-0199-AUDIT.md`（#0001–#0199；151/151 条第三类候选 CI 已逐项核验；#5–#29 的 22 条在主账本，#30–#99 的 53 条、#100–#131 的 24 条、#133–#199 的 52 条分别见 `CI-0030-0099-DETAIL-AUDIT.md`、`CI-0100-0131-DETAIL-AUDIT.md`、`CI-0133-0199-DETAIL-AUDIT.md`；151 次 workflow 均因旧 runner Android SDK Step 4 缺少 `tools` package 失败，Gradle/测试/APK/书源步骤未执行）。
- 历史第 2 册：`docs/required/CI-0200-0399-AUDIT.md`（#0200–#0399；待建立）。
- 后续历史册：#0400–#0599、#0600–#0799、#0800–#0999、#1000–#1199、#1200–#1399、#1400–#1599、#1600–#1799、#1800–#1999、#2000–#2199、#2200–#2399、#2400–#2599、#2600–#2799、#2800–#2999、#3000–#3199，均待按第三类边界核查后建立。
- 第 17 册：`docs/required/CI-3200-3399-AUDIT.md`（#3200–#3399；198/200 实际 run 已逐项登记，165 success / 33 failure；#3398–#3399 无真实 run）。
- 第 18 册：`docs/required/CI-3400-3599-AUDIT.md`（#3400–#3599；已创建，当前 19 次实际 run，16 success / 3 failure / 0 pending）。
- 第 19 册：`docs/required/CI-3600-3799-AUDIT.md`（#3600–#3799；已建立，当前 81 次实际 run，69 success / 12 failure / 0 pending；#3600–#3680 连续无缺号，#3681–#3799 当前未发现实际 run；整册保持开放，后续出现新 run 再追加）。
- 每册只收第三类相关 CI；混合类别要标注交叉边界，纯其他类别 CI 不得计入第三类。历史初筛/元数据登记不等于逐 CI 详细审计完成。
- 原始分段审计文件暂时保留为证据来源：`CI-3200-3299-AUDIT.md`、`CI-3300-3349-AUDIT.md`。新 200 册已吸收现有逐 CI 详细记录；旧账本保留作来源副本，不删除。
- 技术实施历史与详细架构审计仍保留在本文件后续的技术历史文档：`03-LEGADO-RUNTIME.md`（旧版长档案，作为历史证据；日常入口以本总进度文件为准）。CI 功能流水以 200 册为准。

### 每一个 CI 必须逐项填写（不得只写 success/failure）

1. **身份与快照：** CI 编号、attempt、workflow/event、Run ID、分支、完整 exact HEAD SHA、触发时间、conclusion。
2. **Job/Step 矩阵：** 每个 Job/Step 名称、状态、是否实际执行、日志/报告是否存在。
3. **功能覆盖：** 每个成功执行的步骤具体跑通的模块/API/行为/产品功能；测试类型与覆盖边界；功能说明须由断言、日志或报告支持。
4. **失败/跳过：** 每项失败、跳过、取消、pending、未执行步骤；日志证据、直接失败点、已证实根因分类和未验证的下游范围。
5. **书源 E2E：** 源名称/类型、环境/时间、真实请求、搜索/发现/分页/详情/目录/正文/多页/登录/Cookie 实际到达环节；区分 App Runtime E2E、录制回放、外部 Python/HTTP 冒烟。
6. **分层结论：** 分别写 workflow、Job/Step、自动化测试、功能覆盖、真实书源、产品验收结论；禁止用 workflow success 代替功能完成。
7. **证据与复核：** 日志/报告/JUnit/产物链接或路径、Job ID、关键摘要、审计日期、复核状态；写完重新读取账本逐项复核。

**CI 状态纪律：** 不跨 SHA 借用结果；不把 skipped/pending 写通过；不把 continue-on-error 的脚本失败隐藏在 workflow success 下；不把 mock/fixture 当真实网站；不把外部 Python 解析当 Android Runtime；缺日志/报告必须标“证据不可得/未验证”，禁止推测。

The source/build boundary explains the overlap: app default sources contain copies under `app/src/main/java/io/legado/app/data/entities/rule/` and `app/src/main/java/io/legado/app/utils/GsonExtensions.kt`, while `:legado-runtime` separately adds the same seven upstream rule model files and GsonExtensions from the pinned submodule. Both modules produce class files. Representative `javap -p -s` text for SearchRule, BookListRule and GsonExtensionsKt is identical across app/runtime outputs, but their class-file hashes differ. This is a confirmed duplicate-definition packaging anomaly; its runtime class-loader effect was not tested and is not asserted.

### 4.24.1 Task 024 result — Runtime-3.0 ownership and minimum-boundary decision

**Identity and evidence boundary**

- Task 024 registered at start HEAD `802ceb9afcc7fc2afeea07721774a766a58ba0fb`; overview registration commit `c4cc281b5fc338fce4c0e97e3bcc416e3d80299e`; audit registration commit/current start for this review `062c07e85658b950d49dcd2f3229981265b6883d`. Branch: `integration/legado-upstream-runtime`.

- This review used the already-recorded bounded evidence in §§4.20.1–4.23.1 and audit §§24.79.1–24.82.1. The old runner checkout remains detached at source snapshot `4c9cf8ca41a29fcfacdb17fb3ec1d66d4b451343`; it was not treated as the current branch HEAD and no runner operation, source/build inspection, build, test, dependency resolution, CI or device execution was needed.
- Documentation-only result. No production/test/Gradle/source-set/dependency/manifest/schema/database/UI/Reader/license/notice/workflow/APK changes.

**Architecture decision — logical ownership**

| Model/data domain | Single logical owner decision | Runtime and Badcat boundary | Evidence / remaining condition |
|---|---|---|---|
| Pinned rule models (seven rule families) and Gson extensions | `:legado-runtime` is the sole canonical source/compile owner | `:app` consumes the authentic upstream types through its existing runtime dependency; no app-local duplicate declarations | Task 020/021 maps all 38 duplicate DEX names to the seven rule files plus GsonExtensions; no app production caller requires a second copy. Removal is a separate implementation task with app-test and DEX checks. |
| Adjacent source-contract models `RowUi`, `ExploreKind`, `FlexChildStyle` | `:legado-runtime` | Preserve exact pinned types; Badcat owns rendering/forms/screens in `:app` | Task 021 found exact upstream copies and real BaseSource/LoginUiV2/BookSourceExtensions callers. Flexbox/Android dependencies require explicit dependency closure; do not import Legado screens. |
| Authentic `BaseSource`, `BookSource`, `BaseBook`, `Book`, `BookChapter`, `SearchBook`, `AnalyzeRule`, `AnalyzeUrl`, `JsExtensions`, `SharedJsScope`, `JsSourceEngine` | `:legado-runtime` owns the authentic declarations and their source-visible behavior | Runtime passes authentic objects; Badcat DTOs are one-way UI projections only. Android/Room/HTTP/Cookie/WebView/cache/config/crypto/file/coroutine/UI effects cross typed ports | This is the target ownership, not current implementation. Full transitive closure, reflection/script reachability and buildable module closure remain NOT ESTABLISHED. |
| BookSource catalog and check state | One Badcat SourceRepository aggregate is the sole durable owner after migration | Runtime uses authentic BookSource through typed store; catalog UI/import/export remains Badcat | Current FileBookSourceCatalog is the only evidenced catalog owner, but it lacks authentic typed models and check-state transactions. Recommended target storage is a minimal Room-backed repository; migration and parity are not implemented. |
| SearchBook results | Runtime invocation scope; transient by default | Preserve authentic SearchBook and origin while projecting to Badcat search/detail DTOs | No durable SearchBook owner is justified by current caller evidence. If a real pinned caller requires durable search-result storage, register a separate evidence-backed extension; do not persist/merge into Book implicitly. |
| Book and BookChapter metadata/TOC | One Badcat BookRepository/ChapterRepository backed by the same minimal runtime-data store | Runtime owns model semantics; Badcat owns repository implementation and UI projection | No current durable Book/Chapter repository exists. Recommend Room tables for Book and BookChapter with pinned keys/index/FK; exact schema, converters and caller semantics remain open. |
| Reader progress, bookmarks, history | Existing BadcatReadingStateRepository remains sole owner | Runtime accesses through a typed adapter; no second upstream progress writer | Existing SharedPreferences authority remains. Do not move it into Room or duplicate it during this architecture task. |
| Parsed chapter content cache | Existing FileChapterContentCache remains sole owner | Runtime consumes a separate cache port | It is not RuleBigDataHelp and must not be reused for RuleData variables. Preserve its current version-fenced AtomicFile semantics. |
| RuleData large values | One new Badcat RuleVariableStore adapter, filesystem-backed initially | Authentic RuleDataInterface keeps inline/10,000-character/null semantics; typed port supplies only big-value backing | Preserve MD5 namespaces, bookUrl marker, synchronized access, null deletion and cleanup. RSS storage remains outside this book-runtime boundary unless a real BookSource caller proves otherwise. |
| Replacement rules | Existing FileReplaceRuleCatalog remains the sole current owner pending parity | Runtime uses a typed replacement port; Badcat owns editor/import/export UI | Exact pinned scope/order/target/timeout-disable and BookChapter title-normalization parity is unproven; do not claim it is a final compatible owner until differential tests pass. |
| Config | Split ownership: Book.readConfig belongs to the Book record; global source/runtime config belongs to one Badcat host config repository; Reader presentation preferences remain Badcat UI-owned | Runtime receives separate typed per-book and global-config ports | Never conflate Book.readConfig, global ReadBookConfig and Badcat reader preferences. Exact global config field list/serialization owner remains open. |
| Runtime notifications and product actions | Badcat host UI/event services | Typed callback ports only; no Activity/View retained by runtime | Notification failure is distinct from repository commit; preserve cancellation and failure outcomes. |

**Minimum justified storage boundary**

Recommend a dedicated, Badcat-owned Room database boundary for authentic runtime model metadata, not the upstream 26-entity AppDatabase and not an unproven schema freeze. The smallest currently justified relational set is:

1. `book_sources` — authentic BookSource identity and complete rule/config payload, keyed by `bookSourceUrl`.
2. `book_source_check_states` — source check-state keyed/related to BookSource with cascade and transactional begin/complete semantics.
3. `books` — authentic Book identity keyed by `bookUrl`, preserving the pinned unique `(name, author)` contract and typed ReadConfig converter.
4. `chapters` — authentic BookChapter with composite PK `(url, bookUrl)`, unique `(bookUrl, index)`, and FK cascade to Book.

A small host deletion-journal/outbox record is recommended if Book deletion spans Room plus SharedPreferences/filesystem cleanup, so post-commit cleanup can be retried after process death. Its exact representation is a PoC decision, not an approved schema. Room is recommended because the pinned model contracts already expose Room identity/index/FK semantics and chapter/book atomicity; this recommendation does not authorize adding Room now.

Explicitly exclude the upstream AppDatabase registration, unrelated bookshelf groups/memos/highlights/RSS/TTS/audio/video/server entities and product views/DAOs unless a traced BookSource execution caller proves a dependency. Do not include parsed body cache, progress, bookmarks/history, RuleData files or replacement rules in these four model tables. No database migration, schema version or table DDL is frozen by Task 024.

**Module graph and ownership direction**

```text
:app
 ├──> :legado-runtime
 ├──> :integration-contract
 └──> :legado-rhino (direct only where app platform wiring needs it)

:legado-runtime
 ├──> :integration-contract   (model-neutral invocation/platform contracts only)
 └──> :legado-rhino           (authentic Rhino engine/security implementation)

:integration-contract  -X-> :legado-runtime model types
:legado-runtime        -X-> :app
:legado-rhino          -X-> :app / product UI
:legado-runtime-poc    -X-> production dependency graph
```

Model-typed persistence/config/replacement/notification interfaces must live beside the authentic model declarations in `:legado-runtime` (or a future Android-compatible runtime-contract submodule that does not create a cycle). The existing `:integration-contract` remains Kotlin/JVM and model-neutral; it may carry invocation identity, cancellation and generic browser/UI ports but must not import runtime Android/Room/Parcelable model types if Runtime consumes it. `:app` implements runtime-owned typed ports. The graph is a logical recommendation only; exact Gradle variants, transitive dependencies and no-cycle compilation remain unproven.

**Book / BookChapter transaction and deletion protocol**

- **Book write:** load/retain the authentic Book object; validate `bookUrl` and the pinned unique `(name,author)` constraint; write Book and its typed ReadConfig in one repository transaction. Do not create a second Book DTO authority. A failed transaction leaves the previous Book/readConfig unchanged.
- **Chapter batch write:** acquire a per-book write fence; validate every row belongs to the requested `bookUrl`; perform the batch in one Room transaction while preserving PK `(url,bookUrl)`, unique `(bookUrl,index)`, ordering and FK. Apply the exact pinned conflict/replace policy only after the source caller audit; do not invent URL-only matching. Publish cache content only after the corresponding chapter/version fence is current.
- **Book delete prepare:** acquire the per-book lifecycle lock; stop accepting new chapter writes and cancel/quiesce active source/reader jobs; ask the Badcat reader lifecycle port to detach or pause and capture the current progress/bookmark/history snapshot. Do not clear the existing progress owner before the durable delete transaction commits.
- **Book delete commit:** in one Room transaction, record a pending cleanup journal (including the identity and the captured progress snapshot/reference) and delete the Book; the BookChapter FK cascade removes its chapter rows. Any failure before commit rolls back the database mutation and leaves progress/cache/variables available; the reader can be resumed from the snapshot.
- **Post-commit cleanup:** idempotently finalize reader detach, clear only the deleted book's Badcat progress/bookmark/history entries, invalidate its parsed chapter cache, and remove its RuleData big-value namespace. Mark the journal complete only after all cleanup steps succeed. A crash/failure after DB commit is a pending cleanup, not a database rollback; replay the journal on next startup. Keep the record until cleanup succeeds.
- **Source delete/check:** delete BookSource and its check-state in one repository transaction; after commit invalidate live runtime instances and source-scoped caches. Stale check completions must be rejected by a host revision/lease if required by the caller contract; do not claim the lease is an existing upstream token.
- This protocol is a target contract to validate with failure injection and restart tests. It does not claim current behavior or authorize implementation. Exact progress snapshot format and whether reader detach can be safely resumed are open PoC questions.

**Duplicate-class remediation path**

1. In a separately registered source-ownership task, make the seven rule files and GsonExtensions compile only from `:legado-runtime`; remove the redundant app source copies only after verifying all app production/test imports and test compile behavior.
2. Bring exact `RowUi`, `ExploreKind`, `FlexChildStyle` declarations under the same runtime owner when the authentic caller closure is integrated; keep Badcat rendering in app.
3. Add a static duplicate-FQCN guard at module/DEX boundary; require zero duplicate class definitions in the rebuilt APK and compare public JVM descriptors/serialized model fixtures before and after. Do not infer safety from the 29 matching `javap` reports alone.
4. Treat this as a packaging ownership fix only. It does not supply the missing BaseSource/BookSource/BookChapter/AnalyzeRule/AnalyzeUrl/JsExtensions/SharedJsScope/JsSourceEngine source closure.

**Separate implementation/verification tasks required**

- **Task 025 — canonical rule/model source ownership PoC:** exact eight duplicate source removals from app ownership; adjacent three model ownership review; app test/caller migration; Gradle no-cycle and duplicate-FQCN guard; exact APK/DEX zero-duplicate check. No broader runtime source closure bundled into this task.
- **Task 026 — authentic runtime source-closure PoC:** bring in the minimal real BookSource/BaseSource/Book/BaseBook/BookChapter/SearchBook/AnalyzeRule/AnalyzeUrl/JsExtensions/SharedJsScope/JsSourceEngine caller closure from the pinned SHA; typed ports for every platform side effect; no shadow DTO/no-op; exact symbol/caller/API and dependency closure; compile/runtime boundary proof. Keep Legado UI/Reader excluded.
- **Task 027 — minimum storage and typed Host Port PoC:** only after authentic model closure is buildable, implement the minimal Room repository and RuleVariableStore/config/replacement adapters, progress/cache single-owner bridges, transaction/delete journal and migration dry-run/rollback/restart tests. Do not dual-write or migrate user data before a separately accepted migration plan.
- These are proposed task boundaries, not registered implementation authorization. No implementation starts under Task 024.

**Unresolved blockers and gate decision**

- Authentic source closure and caller parity for BaseSource/BookSource/Book/BookChapter/SearchBook/AnalyzeRule/AnalyzeUrl/JsExtensions/inherited groups/SharedJsScope/JsSourceEngine remain NOT ESTABLISHED.
- Exact Gradle dependency/variant closure, Android/Room converters and Flexbox dependency closure, reflection/script-visible reachability, no-cycle compile proof and reproducible packaging remain NOT ESTABLISHED.
- SearchBook durable use, global ReadBookConfig field ownership, replacement-rule parity, BookChapter batch conflict policy, progress snapshot/restore semantics, deletion-journal schema and BookSource file-to-Room migration/rollback require focused PoCs.
- License/SBOM, reproducibility and behavioral differential gates remain open per §4.20.2. The old successful assemble remains bounded evidence only; the 38 duplicate DEX definitions and missing authentic runtime classes are not repaired.
- **Task 024 disposition:** complete as a bounded logical architecture decision and implementation plan. No source/build/test/schema/database/UI changes; no build/test/CI/device/dependency resolution performed.
- **Stage-0:** `in_progress / not accepted`. **Stage-1:** **NO-GO**. Complete Legado compatibility: **NOT ESTABLISHED**. No release readiness claim.
- **Next:** separately preregister Task 025 source ownership remediation and Task 026 authentic source-closure PoC; do not begin storage implementation or Stage-1 until authentic closure and its module boundary are proven.

### 4.27.6 Task 026 Phase A continuation — SharedJsScope and source mutation entry-point closure (2026-11-25)

- **Review HEAD:** `929a3053937e19216ebbee851e3d5a6ad513fb25`; pinned upstream remains `330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a`. Audit details are in §24.89.
- **Shared global invalidation:** source-key deletion calls `ScriptBindings.removeSharedGlobalStatesBySource(sourceClass, MD5(sourceUrl))`, removing source-specific global objects/generation state. `SharedJsScope.remove(jsLib)` is different: it removes the weak cached library scope, URL-backed jsLib file cache entries, and all shared global states under that jsLib hash prefix (potentially affecting multiple sources sharing the library). The former does not remove the library scope; neither operation cancels already-running source scripts by itself.
- **Explore InfoMap:** three layers are now explicit: persistent `CacheManager("infoMap_<sourceUrl>")`, generic cache memory LRU, and `ExploreAdapter.exploreInfoMapList` object LRU (99). Source deletion removes the persisted source-variable/cache rows and generic memory cache prefixes, but no production caller evicts the separate InfoMap object LRU. Exact explicit LRU removal appears only in ExploreRefresh instrumentation teardown. Delete/recreate under the same URL therefore has a static stale-object risk; dynamic manifestation remains unverified.
- **Source mutation callers:** in addition to edit UI, JS upsert and MCP declarative save, pinned production paths include direct `SourceHelp.insertBookSource` imports, HTTP `BookSourceController.saveSource/saveSources`, `RuleUpdate.cacheSource`, legacy import and backup restore direct DAO inserts, and generic BookSource DAO update/metadata paths. Direct DAO insert uses Room REPLACE and does not invoke the same invalidation sequence as the explicit upsert/delete orchestrators. Deletion callers from management, Explore, change-source, HTTP API and MCP converge on SourceHelp cleanup.
- **Host Port refinement:** preserve distinct source-key shared-global invalidation, jsLib-hash-wide scope/file/global invalidation, Explore result invalidation, InfoMap object identity/persistence, and caller-specific direct-insert versus orchestrated-upsert semantics. A centralized lifecycle service would be a deliberate compatibility change requiring differential proof.

### 4.27.7 Task 026 Phase A continuation — DAO update and import/restore/API lifecycle closure (2026-11-25)

### 4.27.67 Task 026Z result — SharedJsScope is Runtime-owned; source execution is not globally serialized (2026-11-26)

- Task 026Z complete for static source/caller/ownership and existing Release DEX reconciliation only. Registration HEAD `2578baba73e3386a204a117148bdd4ce92f68681`; execution source HEAD `2a2f6eb64fa8473bc15be07651c9cc7d6d4999df`; pinned upstream `330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a`. Full result: `/tmp/task026z-shared-scope-port/RESULT.md` SHA-256 `1e92a7b73cbc4e6a4118435359f8e05af2c65ba8827e9b35dee38ea84e5b33f4`.
- **SharedJsScopePort decision:** do not create an App-facing port. Keep shared library scopes, weak/LRU caches, creation locks, CryptoJS owner/thread scopes and ScriptBindings shared-global generations in Runtime/Rhino. Inject only narrow App-owned CryptoJS asset, shared-library fetch, shared-library cache and diagnostics adapters; exact signatures remain open because upstream uses appCtx assets, source-less global OkHttp, ACache and Debug/AppLog.
- **SourceExecutionStatePort correction:** upstream does not globally serialize same-source scripts. BaseSource.evalJS, JsSourceEngine, AnalyzeRule and AnalyzeUrl each create a fresh ScriptBindings/VarScope; the shared jsLib scope is a parent and source globalThis is a separate generation-keyed ConcurrentNativeObject. SourceLock is explicit per-name JS locking; ConcurrentRateLimiter gates requests. Do not introduce a per-source mutex as an assumed upstream behavior.
- **Badcat mismatch:** current RhinoSourceScriptRuntime keeps one persistent ScriptBindings per sourceId and serializes evaluate/getString/putString/clear through a process-static Mutex. It initializes jsLib/mainJs once in that persistent lexical scope and resets on either hash change. This differs from upstream fresh invocation scopes and separate shared library/global state. Current SourceScriptRuntime has no SourceExecutionStatePort or SharedJsScopePort; clearSourceState is a concrete runtime method and catalog invalidation is split between a no-op default and SourceCatalogActivity's separate runtime callback.
- **JS identities:** BaseSource.evalJS binds java/source/sourceApi to the same BaseSource; JsSourceEngine binds java to itself and source/sourceApi to the same BookSource; AnalyzeRule/AnalyzeUrl have their own java objects. One attached BaseSource sidecar can serve source-bound host effects, but each call path must preserve its own bindings/context. JsSourceConfig has no source instance.
- **DEX:** existing upstream Release DEX corroborates fresh JsSourceEngine ScriptBindings allocation/mainJs evaluation and BaseSource identity; no SharedJsScope R8 mapping exists. Source test declarations cover concurrent global writes, library-scope initialization and invalidation but were not executed in this task.
- No build/compiler/test/dependency resolution/DEX/R8/CI/device/network run and no production changes. Phase A remains open; broad BookSource/BaseSource migration blocked; Task 027 blocked; Stage-0 not accepted; Stage-1 **NO-GO**; full compatibility **NOT ESTABLISHED**.
- **Next:** freeze exact App asset/fetch/cache/diagnostic adapters and separate same-object sidecar attachment from source/library invalidation before implementation; preserve fresh per-call scopes and upstream concurrency semantics. No App compile retry under §24.119.

### 4.27.69 Task 026AA result — SharedJsScope host dependencies and BaseSource lifecycle boundary (2026-11-26)

- Task 026AA complete for static adapter/lifecycle contract closure only. Registration/execution HEAD 13bec4238d282adc7760441fa8b069662d515f37; upstream pin 330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a. Full result: /tmp/task026aa-shared-library-host-adapters/RESULT.md SHA-256 f46094aa6976b540a06056c43f04e12ed3aedd6c752e2a6fd5b245bd55217c40.
- **No SharedJsScopePort:** keep scope/cache/lock/generation behavior Runtime-owned. Four App effects need narrow typed adapters: CryptoJS asset text, source-less shared-library HTTP, global shared-library cache and diagnostics. No such production ports exist today.
- **HTTP mismatch/security:** upstream fetch is URL-only GET, no source cookies/headers, status body consumed without status validation, charset/BOM decoding, 60s call timeout and cancellation-aware OkHttp await wrapped in runBlocking without caller context. Its global client follows HTTPS redirects and trusts all TLS certificates/hostnames. Badcat SourceRequestClient is source-scoped, cookie-aware, validates URLs and refuses HTTPS downgrade. Do not reuse as-is or weaken globally; explicit isolated TLS policy decision remains open.
- **Cache:** ACache path cacheDir/shareJs, MD5(URL) logical key, 50,000,000-byte decimal LRU budget, no TTL for these calls. This corrects §24.154/overview §4.27.67 shorthand of 50 MiB. Existing source file cache is sourceId-keyed SHA-256/AtomicFile with a 50 MiB budget and is not a direct substitute.
- **Diagnostics:** Debug.log and AppLog.putDebug are separate channels; AppLog is recordLog-gated and in-memory/event-based; HttpLogInterceptor is separately recordHttpLog-gated and redacted. No diagnostics port exists.
- **Sidecar:** four concrete BaseSource implementers remain BookSource/RssSource/HttpTTS/CoverRule; SourceNavigationBridge is a WebView delegation proxy, not Rhino. There is no production sidecar owner or attach/require method. 026S found 175 direct BookSource DAO references across 62 files, 79 full-entity reads and no central repository/materialization callback. Attach to original runtime-bound objects before execution and reattach after Room/Gson/import/restore/copy; exact central binder remains unproven.
- **Invalidation:** source class+key shared-global invalidation, jsLib-wide scope/cache/global invalidation and product cookie/session/variable/file cleanup are distinct. Existing mutation paths differ: SourceHelp delete clears source state before DAO; same-key edits can direct-delete/insert without source-key clear; jsLib changes remove old library; API/import/restore paths may bypass common cleanup. Active scripts are not cancelled by SharedJsScope invalidation.
- No build/compiler/test/dependency resolution/DEX/R8/CI/device/network run and no production changes. Phase A remains open; broad BookSource/BaseSource migration blocked; Task 027 blocked; Stage-0 not accepted; Stage-1 **NO-GO**; full compatibility **NOT ESTABLISHED**.

### 4.27.85 Task 026AI result — coroutine core and Android dispatcher boundary classified (2026-11-26)

- **Status:** completed for bounded read-only coroutine import/caller and dispatcher ownership classification. Registration start HEAD `d8410f1c207ced590c28a192c81bb2bc9767fe27`; registration/execution HEAD `c23a507714f6667adcc304d3f938ed1021507c4c`; pinned upstream `330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a`.
- **Runtime dependency:** `org.jetbrains.kotlinx:kotlinx-coroutines-core:1.11.0` is a mandatory direct Runtime compile/API candidate. The reused Task 026K candidate matrix contains 44 coroutine import rows across 17 candidate files, including BaseSource, AnalyzeRule/AnalyzeUrl, JsExtensions, SharedJsScope, JsSourceEngine/JsSourceBook, WebBook and content pipeline helpers.
- **Android dispatcher:** `kotlinx-coroutines-android:1.11.0` is an Android host runtime provider, not a replacement for Runtime's direct core dependency. Upstream WebBook and the required `Coroutine.kt` helper default callback execution to `Dispatchers.Main` and use `MainScope`; the final Android App must provide a working Main dispatcher or an exactly equivalent typed dispatcher port. The current Badcat App already declares the Android artifact, but packaging/discovery is not verified.
- **Host lifecycle boundary:** SourceLoginJsExtensions and RssJsExtensions use Activity `lifecycleScope`; RSS navigation switches to Main. Keep these lifecycle/UI owners in App and expose typed callbacks. Do not migrate RSS UI or Activity lifecycle scope into Runtime.
- **Compatibility constraints:** preserve Coroutine<T> callbacks, Main callback defaults, caller CoroutineScope, IO context, optional Semaphore acquire/release, timeout, Flow, Mutex, Job and cancellation propagation. Upstream cancellation handling is not uniform: BaseSource and JsSourceEngine rethrow cancellation; BookChapter returns from its replacement loop; ContentProcessor swallows it in its replacement loop. Do not normalize these behaviors without differential review.

### 4.27.89 Task 026AK result — source image decode, Glide/Media3 and AppCompat lifecycle classified (2026-11-26)

- **Status:** completed for bounded read-only source-vs-host classification. Registration start HEAD `b3da915da7fa81e950e0c3e796fd3acd437cb4d6`; registration/execution HEAD `8bd48539fb52d55990a9b6a2535d62902b55a547`; pinned upstream `330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a`.
- **Source image decode:** `ImageUtils` executes BookSource `coverDecodeJs` for covers and `ContentRule.imageDecode` for content/manga images via `BaseSource.evalJS`, binding `book`, `result` and `src`. Blank rule passes through bytes/stream; script failure logs and returns null. This is source-runtime behavior, not merely Glide UI. `RssSource.coverDecodeJs` is the RSS-specific host branch.
- **Glide boundary:** `AnalyzeUrl.getGlideUrl(): GlideUrl` has one caller (`OkHttpStreamFetcher`) and calls `setCookie()` first. That merges CookieStore cookies and updates the cookie-jar marker header; preserve this source/session side effect. GlideUrl/GlideHeaders, model loader/fetcher, Wi-Fi/manga options, OkHttp callbacks, cancellation and image UI are App adapters. Use a typed resolved-image-request seam; do not make Glide a Runtime dependency.
- **Media3 boundary:** `AnalyzeUrl.getMediaItem(): MediaItem` has only two direct callers, AudioPlayService and ReviewDetailDialog, both playback paths. It calls `setCookie()` and packages URL/headers for ExoPlayer. Keep Media3 1.10.1 and playback helper in App; do not leak MediaItem into Runtime.
- **AppCompat/lifecycle:** JsExtensions.showBrowser uses top AppCompatActivity, UI thread, Activity/FragmentManager lifecycle checks and BottomWebViewDialog. RssJsExtensions/SourceLoginJsExtensions/WebJsExtensions are App bridge classes; SourceLogin inherits the Rss superclass method surface. Their Activity `lifecycleScope`, IO/Main switching, weak references, callback order, clipboard/dialog and RSS/login navigation remain host-owned. `ReviewController.LegacyReviewJsExtensions` also subclasses RssJsExtensions with null Activity and a callback-only browser implementation, so the entire superclass cannot be dismissed as RSS UI.
- **Dependency versions:** pinned App uses Glide 5.0.7 + Glide OkHttp integration/KSP; Media3 1.10.1; AppCompat 1.7.1; Lifecycle 2.10.0. These are App host/playback dependencies, not Runtime dependencies. Inspected current Badcat App/Runtime/Rhino/contract Gradle declarations contain no direct declaration for these four families; no variant/transitive resolution was performed.

### 4.27.91 Task 026AL result — archive/charset JS utility closure classified (2026-11-26)

- **Status:** completed for bounded read-only source/API/dependency classification. Registration start HEAD `0b12f499e013da0643733bff06999a1157f5a5b6`; registration/execution HEAD `fddd9a66318784cb34bf09fc7e38a749aca454b9`; pinned upstream `330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a`.
- **JS API:** `unzipFile`, `un7zFile`, `unrarFile` all alias `unArchiveFile`; the method returns `ArchiveTemp/MD5-16(filename)` and ignores the extracted-file list. ZIP/RAR/7z string methods expose default/explicit charset overloads; public byte-array readers fetch absolute URLs through AnalyzeUrl with source/context or HexUtil-decode local hex input.
- **Archive behavior/security:** ArchiveUtils accepts zip/rar/7z, lazily creates `externalCache/ArchiveTemp` by deleting/replacing an existing temp directory, and extracts to an MD5 filename child. LibArchiveUtils uses native libarchive; ArchivePathUtils rejects absolute/drive/traversal paths and checks canonical containment. Preserve this path security and native handle/stream cleanup.
- **Important source quirks:** JsExtensions.getFile prefixes externalCache but checks containment against `externalCache.parentFile`, so this is not strict cache-root confinement; a `../` path may escape the cache directory while remaining under its parent. ZIP byte extraction appears to advance `nextEntry` twice after nonmatches, potentially skipping alternate entries. EncodingDetect.getEncode(File) increments its sample position only for negative bytes, so ASCII bytes overwrite the current slot. `getTxtInFolder` can call deleteCharAt(-1) for an empty but listable directory. These are static observations, not runtime-tested; do not silently normalize them without differential evidence.
- **Charset/dependencies:** eight vendored ICU Java files in Badcat App are byte-identical to pinned upstream, but remain App-owned; `EncodingDetect.kt` is absent. Pinned App declares libarchive 1.1.6 (Badcat App also declares it), DocumentFile 1.1.0, Core KTX 1.18.0 and Splitties 3.0.0. `LibArchiveUtils` imports Okio Buffer, requiring direct Okio compile ownership if moved verbatim; Runtime has no direct Okio/libarchive edge. Keep RAR/7z capability via a verified Runtime dependency or typed ArchivePort; do not omit it.
- **Product boundary:** generic ArchiveUtils/FileDoc SAF, DownloadManager, local/remote import/export and file-manager callers are product App responsibilities. Only the JS archive surface and required decode semantics belong in the Runtime compatibility contract.
- **Evidence:** `/tmp/task026al-archive-charset/RESULT.md` SHA-256 `b5221a04501976d5cd0ec7ad5b833bf668320f0eca0387b2584fa901ea3f6617`; `callsite-evidence.txt` SHA-256 `0c899436170fe3d5e2f8ed99e6fbe51b5acdd78b4853337492bd592fef050971`; `INPUT-HASHES.sha256` SHA-256 `ff9e3ed522432d7542426621ba0bc069fe61297521413c6fdd78302b97719ce1`; `BADCAT-ICU-HASHES.sha256` SHA-256 `7b8eaa73074e738e5ede08aff9f276fb6ae8702429cf5bcfb8dc27e2e7dd62b6`; all entries in `CHECKSUMS.sha256` passed.
- **Procedure deviation:** registration disclosed limited pre-registration scope discovery using the Task 026K import matrix and JS method-name grep. Substantive source inspection began after registration commit and a full reread of both required lists.
- **Limits:** no Gradle/dependency/variant resolution, download/cache mutation, build/compiler/test/CI/DEX/R8/APK/device/network action; no production/source/test/Gradle/shrinker/schema/UI/Reader/workflow changes; no App compile retry under §24.119. ZIP/charset quirks, native JNI ABI/packaging and Host Port behavior remain NOT ESTABLISHED.

The separate getFile canonical check is not called.

No device path was queried and no filesystem operation/exploit was performed.

- Current Badcat implementation applies a 1–5 alphanumeric suffix filter, safe URL suffix fallback and source-private opaque file token. Existing tests include benign epub/dat and path-argument traversal/symlink cases but no malicious downloadFile type fixture; tests were not executed.
- **Recommendation only:** validate at both download sinks; keep primary missing-type suffix fallback and deprecated missing-type empty result; reject invalid explicit nonblank suffix before network or filesystem mutation; enforce canonical per-source root, symlink defenses and temp-file atomic replacement; never delete old target before success. This changes behavior for unusual explicit type strings. Whether invalid values reject or fall back remains a user decision; policy is NOT approved or implemented.

### 4.27.101 Task 026AQ result — Source Cookie Store identity and lifecycle classified (2026-11-26)

- **Status:** bounded read-only source/API/caller/host persistence closure complete. Registration start HEAD `cffae606d6b64a929b955a3ecb38c83bc0e353c5`; registration/evidence HEAD `f56e542afe561e9d1d445c60cd2524fe66ea7a12`; pinned upstream `330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a`.
- **Identity:** upstream CookieStore is keyed by effective registrable domain (eTLD+1/IP/raw-input fallback), not sourceId. Room Cookie.url is the domain bucket. All sources on the same domain share persistent and session cookie values. BookSource.getKey()/Badcat sourceId is the source URL; getTag() is display metadata. BackstageWebView tag callers pass getKey(). Login header/info are separately source-URL-keyed.
- **Persistence:** persistent cookie values use Room CookieDao plus CacheManager memory; session values use process memory only and override persistent names. Optional encrypted backup covers CookieDao rows but not session cookies; restore merges ordinary domain rows and raw-inserts legacy pipe-key rows. CookieStore.clear() only removes legacy pipe-key rows.
- **JS/API:** CookieStore is bound as cookie in BaseSource.evalJS, JsSourceEngine, AnalyzeUrl and AnalyzeRule; preserve set/replace/get/remove/map conversion and public setWebCookie/getKey/clear, plus separate java.getCookie(tag[,key]) overload. CookieStore.getKey contains a raw-URL session lookup inconsistency; the current Badcat cookie object omits setWebCookie/map converters/clear and adds removeCookie(url,key), which upstream only exposes on CookieManager's internal helper.
- **HTTP:** AnalyzeUrl keys by target request domain and uses enabledCookieJar marker. OkHttp/Cronet load/save through CookieManager on marked exchanges/redirects. Set-Cookie attributes flatten to name/value persistent/session maps; internal marker must not reach origin.
- **WebView:** BackstageWebView asynchronously writes CookieManager values back under source.getKey(); Login/RSS/browser WebViews have separate App paths. Upstream setWebCookie/applyToWebView clears all WebView session cookies globally before setting URL cookies. removeCookie expires visible names for host/eTLD+1 without explicit path/domain. This host behavior needs explicit isolation review.
- **Badcat:** SharedPreferencesSourceCookieJar is shared across sourceId clients and stores values by domain, directionally matching upstream identity. It is not a Runtime port and lacks Room/backup wiring; parser/limits/empty/null/expiry/header-trim semantics differ. DefaultProcessCookieBridge has callback/readback-aware WebView operations but no production callsite; current jar clear does not itself clear WebView cookies. No SourceCookiePort or production Runtime injection exists.
- **Tests:** inspected only: Badcat cookie jar 16, WebView bridge 9, login session 12, Android WebView invalidation 3; pinned upstream backup compatibility 5. None executed.
- **Addendum:** §5.2 SourceCookiePort row and §5.4 cookie compatibility contract updated; SHA-256 `fb48798a1666b8a79de3a5bb96560a5f673d64e74bcadfdf9315a5cc5482120c`.

### 4.27.103 Task 026AR result — CookieStore facade and WebView invalidation contract classified

- **Status:** bounded read-only API/caller/WebView invalidation review complete. Registration start HEAD ec569c071338ff5fd5b172e80ef944498196a87f; registration commit / substantive inspection HEAD dae68458278cc479c60f9d17335034227307f5ce; pinned upstream 330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a.
- **CookieStore API:** 026P did not inventory the separately bound CookieStore object. Its source contract is six CookieManagerInterface methods (setCookie, replaceCookie, getCookie, removeCookie, cookieToMap, mapToCookie) plus three public CookieStore methods (setWebCookie, getKey, clear). This is distinct from java.getCookie(tag[,key]) and from CookieManager.removeCookie(url,key).
- **Badcat facade:** RhinoSourceCookieExtensions exposes six methods but omits cookieToMap/mapToCookie/setWebCookie/clear; narrows setCookie nullability; and adds the wrong-namespace removeCookie(url,key) overload. Existing RhinoSourceScriptRuntimeTest explicitly asserts that extra overload. CookieStore.clear only deletes legacy CookieDao OkHttp rows; it must not be mapped to Badcat's full clearDomain.
- **WebView:** upstream setWebCookie/applyToWebView call removeSessionCookies(null) globally before setting one URL's pairs. This can remove unrelated WebView session cookies across all origins in the process; the source does not await callbacks, flush or read back. CookieStore.removeCookie(url) is a separate domain-store clear plus host/eTLD+1 visible-name expiry. BackstageWebView separately imports page-finish cookies asynchronously under source.getKey().
- **Badcat wiring:** DefaultProcessCookieBridge has callback-aware URL sync and bounded domain/path invalidation, but repository search found no production construction/callsite. No SourceCookiePort declaration exists. SharedPreferencesSourceCookieJar has no Android CookieManager operations; the primary factory wires only the domain store callbacks. SourceCatalogActivity's separate runtime has only read/remove/clear callbacks.
- **Policy:** exact global upstream purge, narrower URL/domain expiry, and no implicit purge/explicit blocked operation were compared. CookieManager.getCookie does not expose original cookie attributes, so scoped expiry cannot reliably isolate session-only cookies or guarantee complete path coverage. No policy was selected; user decision remains required.
- **Evidence:** /tmp/task026ar-cookie-facade-webview/RESULT.md SHA-256 9de427f1fa8f5ac0931e93a330116f0f11964b1e9189e46d669d7f1a16e3fc1a; cookie API matrix 264c3e83d6a3b0f033acf7dd091883aea98e0f5e3e215c35317b8148964b1716; invalidation matrix 7ee68a82e32ecc22c6b93e84a865a5756b4316e558f36bd9b7f111d501bb255d; Badcat gap matrix 38c08616900b096687d262ad68cd6feac10463e3e07e1c90521fe8bb99ba014a; input manifest 845b1407d0520072ab331c811934c12f3f8a0f045b11f374cd61a83d473fcba2; 18 source/document/test inputs; checksums passed.
- **Validation boundary:** tests inspected only; none executed. No Gradle/dependency resolution/build/compiler/test/CI/DEX/R8/APK/device/network action; no production/source/test/Gradle/shrinker/schema/UI/Reader/workflow change; no App compile retry. ### 4.27.105 Task 026AS result — Hidden WebView SSL/mixed-content trust boundary classified (2026-11-26)

- **Status:** bounded read-only source/caller/security review complete. Registration start HEAD `4225694d321fc181829e866feb2dfb32924831f9`; registration/execution HEAD `bb90bf905ed6adcaa6d2a902dfb6def76ff6b007`; pinned upstream `330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a`.
- **Pooled policy:** pinned WebViewPool globally enables JavaScript, DOM storage, `MIXED_CONTENT_ALWAYS_ALLOW`, media playback without gesture and content URI access. It is shared by hidden source execution, visible browser, RSS, BookInfo, video, login and dialog surfaces. Release resets selected JS/UI/cache-mode/viewport fields but does not explicitly clear cookies, DOM storage, WebView cache/history or reset mixed-content/content/file-access settings. Cross-feature state leakage is a static risk, not dynamically demonstrated.
- **SSL:** BackstageWebView Html/Sniffer clients proceed on any SSL error while the view remains owned by the active context, otherwise cancel. Browser, RSS, login and BottomWebViewDialog clients unconditionally proceed regardless of the error. No certificate-error classification or user confirmation exists. This accepts invalid server identity and creates MITM exposure for page content, source scripts, cookies and login credentials.
- **Mixed content/content access:** pooled views allow HTTP subresources inside HTTPS pages and content URI access. File access is not explicitly assigned by WebViewPool; effective value needs API-level runtime verification. CodeEditActivity has a separate stricter WebView with file/content access disabled.
- **Manifest distinction:** upstream targetSdk 36 has a network-security base config allowing cleartext and debug-only user/system trust anchors. Badcat targetSdk 36 sets `usesCleartextTraffic=true` and has no networkSecurityConfig reference. Cleartext permission is separate from accepting SSL certificate errors.
- **Badcat gap:** no production BackstageWebView/WebViewPool/SSL override/mixed-content implementation found. SourceBook and ReaderCss WebViews disable JavaScript and file/content access; they are not the source hidden WebView or visible browser/login/RSS integration.
- **Options:** A reproduce upstream permissive behavior (best compatibility, highest security risk); B strict SSL/mixed-content/file/content defaults (safer, may break legacy sources); C separate WebView profiles by trust boundary with strict TLS baseline and narrowly scoped mixed-content capability (more work, better isolation). No policy was selected. Recommendation for review: do not retain unconditional SSL proceed as default; prefer strict certificate validation and assess mixed-content exceptions per source.
- **Separation:** Task 026AR global session-cookie clearing remains a distinct unresolved choice. The already selected SharedJsScope-only trust-all/HTTPS-downgrade client does not authorize permissive WebView TLS.
- **Evidence:** `/tmp/task026as-webview-security/RESULT.md` SHA-256 `6d93f49e373d1d8b1597774671769f079e31a3c5f8ad954e60cd658e283f1bb6`; input manifest `d5c098eb12d05e449c0819fd18ffb74e9e11e24bac7695025f40b66e841b21d5` (18 files); security matrix `d875180c63eb0fd67062eafcb0874371127690b65f6c964b32cf125326172217`; pool caller matrix `df397118e64e3fe014f8eb3094d6503b1da474684bea29e8c142ff00b21c1ee3`; checksums passed.
- **Validation boundary:** no tests/build/CI/Gradle/dependency resolution/DEX/R8/APK/device/network/WebView execution; no App compile retry; no production/source/test/Gradle/manifest/schema/UI/Reader/workflow changes. `git diff --check` passed.
- **Disposition:** Task 026AS complete for static policy review only. WebView SSL/mixed-content policy remains not approved/not implemented. Phase A open; broad migration blocked; Task 027 blocked; Stage-0 not accepted; Stage-1 **NO-GO**; full compatibility **NOT ESTABLISHED**.
- **Next:** obtain explicit user approval before registering production WebView security/profile changes. Runtime variant resolution/compile still requires a clean higher-capacity runner; no App compile retry.

### 4.27.106 User decision — WebView security policy Option A approved

- The user explicitly selected **Option A** from Task 026AS: reproduce the pinned upstream permissive WebView behavior for the hidden BackstageWebView and visible browser/RSS/login/dialog surfaces.
- Approved parity includes unconditional SSL-error `proceed()` behavior, pooled `MIXED_CONTENT_ALWAYS_ALLOW`, JavaScript/DOM storage enabled, `allowContentAccess=true`, and the existing shared WebViewPool reuse/release behavior.
- `allowFileAccess` was not explicitly set by upstream WebViewPool; this decision does not newly enable it. It also does not authorize universal file-URL access, new JavaScript interfaces, third-party cookies, new cleartext exceptions, or any broader weakening.
- This is an acknowledged compatibility-over-security tradeoff: invalid TLS identity can be accepted, HTTPS pages may load modifiable HTTP subresources, and pooled state may persist across trust contexts. It does not alter ordinary source HTTP TLS, the separately approved SharedJsScope client boundary, or current isolated Reader/CSS preview WebViews.
- Decision recording is not implementation and does not close Phase A. Any production change needs a separately preregistered task and validation. Task 026AR's session-cookie clearing decision remains independent and unresolved.

### 4.27.108 Task 026AT result — AnalyzeUrl option/caller and HTTP transport gap closed statically (2026-11-26)

- **Status:** bounded read-only source/API/caller review complete. Registration start HEAD `fe7d895fb480bce2d6d3c45a369cbf89653af3ed`; registration/execution HEAD `3c66ecef706f7241f6ad54a839deba6faf94d769`; pinned upstream `330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a`.
- **Pinned UrlOption:** 16 fields: method, charset, headers, body, origin, retry, type, webView, webJs, timeout, followRedirects, dnsIp/resolveIp, js, bodyJs, serverID, webViewDelayTime. URL JS/template/page expansion occurs before comma-JSON parsing; source headers are loaded first, source `proxy` is extracted from source header config, then URL-option headers overlay.
- **Material mismatches:** upstream maps only POST/HEAD specially and defaults all other methods to GET; Badcat executes additional PUT/PATCH/PROPPATCH/REPORT methods. Upstream charset controls query/form encoding and `escape`, not response decoding; Badcat rejects `escape` and applies named charset to response. Upstream source proxy and same-site login-header semantics are absent. Upstream retry retries all non-success/non-3xx responses regardless of method, while Badcat retries selected transient failures only for GET/HEAD. Upstream timeout changes read + derived call timeout; Badcat sets connect/read/write/call equally. Upstream `dnsIp`, WebView, type, origin/serverID and delay behaviors are missing or separately product-owned. `js`/`bodyJs` are not equivalent; production `java.ajax` direct lambdas bypass gateway stages.
- **Request/response contract:** current SourceHttpRequest/Response cannot represent proxy/DNS, raw OkHttp Response, StrResponse metadata, live InputStream, multipart upload, data URI, or complete WebView/result flow. Upstream has separate string/raw response/byte/stream/upload paths and data-URI handling. Glide/Media3 remain App adapters per 026AK.
- **Caller inventory:** 60 direct AnalyzeUrl constructor references in 32 pinned source files. Source search/explore/detail/TOC/content/rule/JS/image/dictionary paths are Runtime candidates; browser/RSS/review/audio/video/Glide/WebDAV/config/import/local-book and other product callers remain App-owned unless proven otherwise. `UrlOptionDialog` and `CurlAnalyzeUrlConverter` are partial App editing helpers, not full runtime parser coverage.
- **TLS correction:** upstream `HttpHelper.okHttpClient` is globally configured with unsafe trust manager/hostname verifier and SSL downgrade redirects enabled. `getProxyClient(null)` returns it, and `AnalyzeUrl.getClient()` uses `getProxyClient(proxy)`. Therefore normal upstream AnalyzeUrl requests and SharedJsScope library downloads share the permissive global client. The user-selected Badcat policy remains intentionally narrower: trust-all/HTTPS downgrade only for the dedicated SharedJsScope client; ordinary Badcat source HTTP stays strict. This is an explicit security/compatibility divergence from pinned upstream, not exact parity. Do not broaden ordinary source HTTP without a separate user decision. This is distinct from the WebView Option A decision in 026AS.
- **Evidence:** `/tmp/task026at-analyzeurl-request-options/RESULT.md` SHA-256 `0cf5527184d564d8c5ab7fc4343eb1bed8a5eaf624b5787d432eba81c3245c25`; option matrix `957369a83fcc9ba5f116065f8849fbdd4a61f8b28811bb3d949cfbf413cd282e`; caller matrix `b8866aa83ced51c930aa90bb5c9101b6a245796bee551e23c9bf76c849017b02`; callsite inventory `7a229a834045f924d641d28174e503093151f3114d15db0731628f09e0f6064f`; input manifest `0a262523191fd2f666a51caaea5bd3de6b3be20ef9c4fcc1aee5903448d6cb03` (51 source/test files).
- **Validation:** no tests/build/CI/Gradle/dependency resolution/DEX/R8/APK/device/network/live request; no App compile retry; no production/source/test/Gradle/manifest/schema/UI/Reader/workflow changes. `git diff --check` passed.

- **API/callers:** five 026P declarations, all unannotated Android JavascriptInterface but Rhino-visible: queryBase64TTF, queryTTF with/without useCache, replaceFont with/without filter. ruleHelp.md demonstrates base64 and URL font parsing and replaceFont(..., true). No Kotlin production caller was found; this is a source-script API.
- **Parser:** QueryTTF.java in upstream and Badcat App is byte-identical (SHA-256 09be94396c2ac0ad85c8a8619cb7220f56156c8fcc0ea0f6ce3607c536961709). It parses name/head/cmap/loca/maxp/glyf, cmap formats 0/4/6, and builds mutable Unicode/glyph maps. No fontbox dependency. It lacks format 12/CFF/TTC support and robust table-offset/bounds validation; parser itself has no size limit.
- **Upstream cache/input:** String URL fetch uses AnalyzeUrl with same BaseSource and active CoroutineContext; non-URL String uses Android Base64 decoder; ByteArray accepted; actual code has no local File branch despite KDoc. String cache key hashes original input string and is checked before fetch/decode; ByteArray key hashes bytes. Global AppCacheManager LruCache capacity 4, no TTL/font invalidation. queryTTF logs exceptions and rethrows; deprecated queryBase64TTF logs a warning then delegates.
- **Badcat differences:** accepts extra source-file/token input; caps font bytes at 8 MiB; content-hash/sourceId cache of eight entries is checked only after fetch/decode, so cache hits still make network requests; source-scopes mutable parser objects; no explicit invalidation. URL fetch uses the injected sourceGet path with WebUrlResolver/SourceRequestFactory, 15s timeout, retry >=1, redirects and non-2xx rejection, runBlocking(IO) without script CoroutineContext cancellation; only BadcatReadingEngineFactory wires this callback. Upstream uses AnalyzeUrl/full context and returns raw response bytes without 2xx-only filtering. Badcat queryTTF omits upstream exception logging; queryBase64TTF omits deprecation marker/log.
- **replaceFont mismatch:** when an error-font glyph exists but the correct font has no reverse glyph mapping, upstream preserves the original character even with filter=true; Badcat drops it. Missing error-font glyph filtering and blank-codepoint preservation otherwise match the reviewed logic.
- **Owner boundary:** QueryTTF parser/remapping/cache semantics are Runtime behavior; source HTTP and diagnostics are typed App ports; optional local file support uses SourceFilePort. AndroidX Collection LruCache is a dependency candidate already classified in 026AH/026AM. No SourceFontCachePort is currently implemented; Runtime can own the LRU unless host-managed cache policy is explicitly selected.
- **Evidence:** /tmp/task026av-font-utility/RESULT.md SHA-256 4e5289391225743d25ce0e750cb251c8bc92b8e831cbde57f679ad44c0444c95; declaration matrix 57a00445917bb090c115fb703c37d91ebd2bac90221ac0460711e3a007d3596e; script caller matrix 3058525808ff00a8a15c0293611f82098fa9d53905b4fd7be83c2f412e49200c; input manifest 03c9867b16ff6fd3f0366b49f4b681c27b2e46e73dfccd133c3875ee68fdc85a; checksums passed.
- **Validation:** tests and parser source were inspected only; no test/font parse/live request executed. No build/CI/Gradle/dependency resolution/DEX/R8/APK/device/network; no App compile retry; no production/source/test/Gradle/shrinker/schema/UI/Reader/workflow changes.

Debug and AppLog are separate channels.

These are App host/product actions; selected WebView Option A remains unchanged.
- **Refresh events:** the three methods post distinct Boolean true LiveEventBus tags. BookInfo observes info/TOC, ReadBook observes TOC/content; observers act only when lifecycle is STARTED. Events are global/fire-and-forget with no source/book identity in the payload.
- **Badcat gaps:** current bridge implements injected androidId/toast/UA and pure string-return log/logType only. log has no Debug/AppLog side effects, maps null to empty and returns String instead of original Any?; logType returns String instead of Unit. Main reading factory injects providers but uses Toast.makeText and Settings.Secure.getString(..., Settings.Secure.ANDROID_ID).orEmpty(); upstream uses Settings.System.getString(..., Settings.Secure.ANDROID_ID) ?: "null", so both API namespace and fallback differ. Login/catalog/default gateway runtime roots omit providers and use throwing defaults plus hardcoded Android 12/Chrome 120 UA. No current openUrl/showBrowser/openVideoPlayer/refresh methods.

HostUiPort/ProductEventPort/VisibleBrowserPort and HostUiAction/ProductRefreshEvent are declarations only; no production implementers/wiring were found. Existing SourceExecutionContext has source identity and Job but not full upstream CoroutineContext/navigation suppression state. Product UI/player/event observers remain App-owned.

### 4.27.116 Task 026AX result — reading/theme configuration JS snapshots classified (2026-11-26)

- **Status:** bounded static API/payload/caller/Host Port review complete. Registration start HEAD b994f556fb0cbb60885440b3d7b0b368bdb5a660; registration/substantive inspection HEAD de3747bee4920bbd144ab3f63b5d137ede4671c7; pinned upstream 330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a.
- **Declarations:** five methods: getReadBookConfig String (annotated), getReadBookConfigMap Map<String,Any> (unannotated), getThemeMode String (annotated), getThemeConfig String (annotated), getThemeConfigMap Map<String,Any?> (unannotated). All are Rhino-visible; no overloads.
- **Read config selection:** durConfig selects comicStyleSelect when isComic, otherwise readStyleSelect; getConfig resets when fewer than five configs remain and falls back to configList[0] for invalid index. Even with shareLayout true, these JS methods expose durConfig, not the shared-layout config property.
- **Read config payloads differ:** JSON serializes the 82-property Config object, excludes @Transient derived color caches and omits null template fields by Gson default. Map creates a fresh 82-key projection, omits six nullable tip templates but includes six transient textColorInt/textAccentColorInt cache values (possibly -1), and reuses the reviewIconSvgTemplates list reference. Kotlin Map is read-only by declaration; Rhino mutability is untested.
- **Theme mode:** preference default "0"; AppConfig listener updates cached themeMode, isEInkMode and ResourceThemeGeneration. Values 1/2/3 mean light/dark/e-ink; unknown values are returned unchanged while isNightTheme falls back to system mode. The JS Elvis fallback is redundant for non-null String.
- **Theme config:** each getDurConfig call creates a fresh nine-field snapshot from current day/night preferences and Android resource defaults. E-ink is non-night and uses the day branch. Theme Map retains null backgroundImgPath; Gson JSON omits it. getDurConfig reads active preferences/resources, not the saved theme preset list.
- **Callers:** exact-name search found only declarations, jsHelp documentation and code-completion entries; no shipped default-data script invocation. Badcat bridge has none of the five methods.
- **Badcat Host Port gap:** RuntimeHostPort declares neither RuntimeConfigPort nor ResourcePort. No production config/resource adapter or wiring exists. Main reading, catalog, login, gateway, login-service and URL-evaluator runtime construction roots must all be considered; several can construct default runtimes that do not receive host callbacks.
- **Owner split:** Runtime owns JS API/snapshot orchestration; App owns ReadBookConfig selected state/files/transient caches, AppConfig preference/listener state, ThemeConfig preference resolution and Android resource defaults. Keep Reader/theme UI and full AppConfig/ThemeConfig out of Runtime. RuntimeConfigPort remains a proposal, not an implementation or frozen signature. Other ResourcePort needs elsewhere remain separate.

### 4.27.118 Task 026AY result — source-visible browser-await and verification callback closure (2026-11-26)

- **Status:** complete for bounded read-only source/API/caller/Host Port closure. Registration start HEAD 742dd6e643d01c4b22cfa20c49dccd180d58ee9b; registration/execution HEAD f6ef9dd23db3dfdd0f9ab3c7db742a41086152b7; pinned upstream 330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a.
- **API:** six unannotated Rhino-visible declarations: startBrowser (2), startBrowserAwait (3), getVerificationCode (1). startBrowser is launch-only; startBrowserAwait returns StrResponse; getVerificationCode returns String. jsHelp's documented two-argument refetch default is false, but the actual two-argument overload delegates with true.
- **Coordinator:** SourceVerificationHelp serializes verification UI globally, assigns UUID per attempt, keeps one-shot URL/body results, and attaches a close callback. Cancellation/interruption removes the attempt and closes the Activity on main; close waits at most five seconds. The owner wait has no absolute timeout.
- **Single-flight:** only browser + refetch=true + html=null calls coalesce. Key is source type/source key/scheme/host/port, excluding path/query. Followers wait up to five minutes and then refetch their own URL; abandoned owners allow retry/election. Supplied HTML, image-code and capture-only paths do not coalesce.
- **Results:** refetch mode reuses supplied initial html or performs a source/context-aware AnalyzeUrl fetch; capture-only mode returns documentElement.outerHTML and current WebView URL. Image-code OK returns entered text; cancel/empty becomes NoStackTraceException.
- **Badcat:** BrowserVerificationRequest/Result, VerificationCodeRequest and VisibleBrowserPort/VerificationCodePort declarations exist but have no production implementers or injection. No VerificationCoordinator or Rhino bridge methods exist. ShowBrowserDialog is the separate 026AW showBrowser surface. Current DTO timeout/validation/default/result shape differs from upstream; SourceExecutionContext has Job and navigation enum but not full CoroutineContext/SuppressSourceNavigation and requires nonblank sourceName.
- **Scripts/tests:** no shipped default script invocation found. ruleHelp's getVerificationCode() is a Login UI callback, not java.getVerificationCode. Six upstream unit tests and one blocked-navigation instrumentation test were inspected only, not executed.

### 4.27.120 Task 026AZ result — direct JsExtensions HTTP API and caller closure (2026-11-26)

- **Status:** complete for bounded read-only source/API/caller/Host Port closure. Registration start HEAD c8f9c1131e58540f18a9b7fe794c107c65cf95c6; registration/execution HEAD ed31e0b4c08d5990a652c8ce287857aa1d1978a6; pinned upstream 330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a.
- **API:** 15 unannotated Rhino-visible declarations: ajax (2), ajaxAll (2), ajaxTestAll (2), connect (3), get (2), head (2), post (2). ajax/connect/batch methods use AnalyzeUrl; get/head/post use Jsoup directly. ajax/connect actual timeout type is Long? despite jsHelp documenting Int?.
- **ajax:** current bridge has no callTimeout overload, partial URL-option handling only, a 15s default/30s cap, 2xx-only acceptance and 8 MiB body cap; upstream returns body for non-2xx by default, logs errors and rethrows cancellation after ensureActive.
- **Batch:** upstream uses AppConfig.threadCount (default 32), active CoroutineContext, mapAsync and ordered results; Badcat uses fixed max concurrency 4 and sourceGet/GET callbacks without per-URL AnalyzeUrl options. ajaxTestAll's production adapter loses the original exception cause before the bridge maps -1..-7.
- **connect:** upstream uses AnalyzeUrl URL-option method/body and optional header JSON; Badcat always sends GET. The shipped bookSources.json login example uses connect with URL-option POST/body.
- **get/head/post:** upstream Jsoup returns Connection.Response, no redirects, 30s default, explicit headers and CookieJar marker; Badcat returns RhinoSourceHttpResponse, resolves relative URLs, merges source/login headers, retries GET/HEAD and returns non-2xx status values rather than Jsoup's default error exception behavior. Badcat POST content type is inferred by its request encoder.
- **Security:** upstream AnalyzeUrl inherits global unsafe TLS from 026AT; Jsoup get/head/post use SSLHelper's trust-all certificate socket factory. Badcat ordinary SourceRequestClient remains strict TLS as explicitly chosen; do not broaden TLS.
- **Callers:** source template uses ajax; coverRule uses ajaxAll; httpTTS uses ajaxTestAll; bookSources uses connect URL-option POST; help docs cover get/head/post. No Kotlin production helper caller found. Existing Badcat tests were inspected only, not run.

### 4.27.124 Task 026BB result — JavaImporter JCE, digest and byte-array API closure (2026-11-26)

- **Status:** complete for bounded static JCE/digest/Base64/byte-array closure. Registration start HEAD a67c25fed5f1ec45a9f67418f8fbd87dcdd19ef7; registration/execution HEAD 75f5507613a9070216b2001eed1d4baa96cdd1bb; pinned upstream 330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8ab7a4132c784da9a.
- **Upstream boundary:** Badcat's RhinoClassShutter, RhinoWrapFactory and CatchableNativeJavaObject are byte-identical to pinned upstream. ClassShutter does not block JCE, MessageDigest, Android/Java Base64, URLEncoder or String; it blocks sensitive file/reflection APIs and cn.hutool.core.io.
- **Demand:** pinned AndroidJsTest exercises JavaImporter class arguments, HmacSHA1 Mac, SecretKeySpec, String.getBytes("UTF-8"), Android Base64.NO_WRAP and URLEncoder; DatatypeConverter is imported only. External sources require AES/DES/DESede, DESKeySpec/SecretKeyFactory, MessageDigest MD5, Java/Android Base64, Arrays copy methods and String byte constructors.
- **Badcat gap:** the shim only exposes AES/CBC/PKCS5Padding, AES SecretKeySpec/IvParameterSpec, limited Java Base64/Arrays and Jsoup.parse. JavaImporter class arguments and instance importClass are not compatible. Mac, SecretKeyFactory, DESKeySpec, MessageDigest, Android Base64, URLEncoder and Java String byte constructors are absent.
- **Byte arrays:** current Cipher.doFinal returns a UTF-8 toString wrapper, not byte[]; Java Base64 encoder converts arbitrary bytes through UTF-8 and can corrupt them; Arrays.copyOfRange rejects Java's zero-padding case; String.getBytes ignores charset. Current input/output cap is 2 MiB.
- **Security/owner:** no Host Port is needed; use typed Runtime JCE facades only. DES/DESede/MD5/HmacSHA1 are source-demanded legacy algorithms, not blanket security approval. Do not expose generic reflection. IoUtil remains denied by upstream ClassShutter; ArrayUtil is separate scope.
- **Tests:** current Badcat tests cover only AES and rejection boundaries; no JCE parity tests. Inspected only, not run.

### 4.27.134 Task 026BG result — batch-content and source-navigation context Host Port closure (2026-11-27)

- **Status:** complete for bounded read-only contract closure. Registration start HEAD e55f1458a48fdd6b786763b1544b6431bfd61025; registration/execution HEAD ad1a3a98bdb4b60d65a0df1b8d6bb6a5b7b3f4a1; pinned upstream 330e606c4d57e08adc056ff8b7a4132c784da9a.
- **Required lists:** Master Workflow (365 lines) and Runtime Overview (2,379 lines) were fully reread from registration HEAD ad1a3a98bdb4b60d65a0df1b8d6bb6a5b7b3f4a1 before substantive inspection.
- **Batch behavior:** standard BookContent and JS JsSourceBook paths each create a BatchContentContext with a CoroutineContext and per-chapter save tokens, execute contentBatch/getContentBatch, close in finally and return missing chapters. Batches require at least two chapters and max size is capped at 50. Chapter identity is index; unique original/absolute/normalized URLs are aliases; duplicate URLs are ambiguous; numeric identifiers are rejected.
- **Save lifecycle:** cacheContent is synchronous and Boolean-returning; it applies per-chapter replacement, validates a save token and writes through BookHelp. Partial successes survive script failure; missing chapters fall back to single fetch; CacheBook later saves images and posts success. Late callbacks after close fail. Outer CacheBook tokens and inner BatchContentContext tokens are captured separately; current tests do not cover edits in the gap, so future Host design should share one token snapshot or prove safety.
- **Navigation behavior:** suppression is operation-local CoroutineContext marker AND live blockSourceNavigation preference. AnalyzeRule/JsSourceEngine/AnalyzeUrl preserve context with dispatcher handling; WebView carries block.context into a DAO-reloaded source proxy. openUrl/openVideoPlayer/browser UI are gated; login and HTTP remain functional.
- **Badcat gaps:** no batch engine or BatchContentPort; contentBatch rejected by CSS parser; BookSource only retains raw rule JSON and maxBatchSize is only integer-validated; current cache is single-chapter. RhinoSafeSourceJavaExtensions returns null for getBatchContext, throws from cacheContent and returns EmptyCoroutineContext for navigation. SourceExecutionContext is Job plus enum only and has no production constructor; HostUiPort is declaration-only.
- **Candidate contract:** App Host owns Book/BookChapter/Room/BookHelp/tickets/images; a one-use BatchContentSession exposes only synchronous cacheContent, missing indexes and close. Its sync callback vs suspend cache requires an explicit cancellable bridge. SourceNavigationContextPort carries per-operation marker and parent Job; HostUiPort checks current preference at dispatch. Do not expose full BatchContentContext entities or raw Activity/View.
- **Evidence:** RESULT.md SHA-256 64e3bcc3ab550419261d21ded5b141fe1bfd5f2f29a103993e12f68f52327e0c; batch method/caller matrix 30cb3563f7997fac12d12e16e193bc473134057dc1d8e6f2af1e2e96f4945f46; batch lifecycle matrix 3a3f3d9e4bc09fce4d07586416ca9831c50b2328b6e837654d41da108b610fc9; navigation flow matrix 06d803982ffbcd8b138a707b0e14e443323ac8bab2f0babd1c6c345f61a4359a; Host Port gap matrix 94443738371c37a43a2d42cc8901fc9bd9a3c3f4a86efc160cfc9a41a86f002a; Host Port contract 93eaea4d0b5cdf2727ac59a364f4b04ad932a450f00e0f0a4a6eb3512b7d24fe; policy tests 2e4311b66ceeb940f638d46ba164b9f3dccd4244adafc3f1ecab187a044e5996; input manifest a27f1cebf8aebb404d11327426d54e5cb8a84a2d68cfbbb55400fcfaf2f2831f; all checksums passed.
- **Boundary:** no build/test/Gradle/dependency/CI/DEX/R8/APK/device/network/source execution; no App compile retry; no production/source/test/Gradle/schema/database/UI/Reader/workflow changes.
- **Disposition:** Phase A remains open; batch-content/navigation parity NOT IMPLEMENTED; broad BookSource/BaseSource migration and Task 027 blocked; Stage-0 not accepted; Stage-1 NO-GO; full Legado compatibility NOT ESTABLISHED.

### 4.27.150 Task 026BO scope amendment — do not invent raw-source SPDX checksums

- 026BN stores Git blob SHA-1 values and SHA-256 hashes of first-100-line header excerpts, not complete raw source files. A Git blob object ID is not a raw-file checksum; an excerpt hash is not a whole-file checksum. Do not place either in an SPDX File checksum field.
- The file-level source/license deliverable is therefore a separate checksum-verified FILE-LICENSE-EVIDENCE.tsv sidecar with 123 C translation-unit rows and eight relevant-header rows. It must label Git blob object IDs and header-excerpt hashes accurately, include exact license classifications/exceptions and source commit/tree, and reconcile one-to-one with the 026BN matrices.
- Add source-only SPDX package nodes with filesAnalyzed=false where full source bytes are unavailable; keep libarchive aggregate license NOASSERTION because of per-file exceptions. Use extracted LicenseRef text only where exact custom notice text is present. Do not fabricate SPDX File objects or raw-source checksums.
- Keep the four AAR ELF payloads separate, with exact AAR member paths, sizes, SHA-256 and Build IDs, but NOASSERTION for native license/source relationship. Do not assert a binary-to-source relationship.
- Validate SPDX package/relationship/custom-license structure and sidecar coverage; explicitly report the unavailable raw-source checksums. All other 026BO boundaries and exclusions remain unchanged.
